Are there amendments (Request for Change) for Vehicle Sales Agreement?

Do you collaborate with external partners such as Channel Partners or Manufacturer's Reps on
agreements/forecasts?

How many stages exists in Vehicle Lifecycle?
How are Vehicles created currently inside the system?
Where is the Vehicle Master today?

How will households/relationships be maintained?
How do you intend on splitting households?
How do you plan on merging households?

Do different user groups need to see different information in the console?
Do you want console users to connect with customers or website visitors in real time through text-
only live chats?

Do you have important customer information that agents must see during the conversation at all
times?

phone system or different ones? If different, please specify
Is Service Cloud Console going to be used?
What data should an agent see when a call comes in? Any data coming in from external systems?

How many Vehicles types are quoted by individual Quote?
Is Quote Comparison needed as part of shopping experience?
How many pricing attributes are part of the Quoting process?
How many products are in your catalog?
How are your price books set up today?
Single Pricebook vs Multiple Price books?
Single Currency vs Multiple Currencies. How many currencies?
Where is the Product Master today?

Is a Customer Experience Cloud in scope for Life Insurance as part of this engagement?
Does the customer want to include mid-term adjustments in the Life Insurance Customer Experience
Cloud site?
Should renewal information be available in the Life Insurance Customer Experience Cloud site?
Should policy documents be available in the Life Insurance Customer Experience Cloud site?
Should claim information be available in the Life Insurance Customer Experience Cloud site?
Is a Customer Hosted site (API approach) in scope for Life Insurance as part of this engagement?
Does the customer want to include mid-term adjustments in the Customer Hosted site?
Should renewal information be available in the Customer Hosted site?
Should policy documents be available in the Customer Hosted site?
Should claim information be available in the Customer Hosted site?
Is a Broker/Agency Experience Cloud site for Life Insurance in scope for this engagement?
Should a Broker/Agency have the ability to quote Life Insurance from the Experience Cloud site?
Should a Broker/Agency have the ability to make Life Insurance purchases on the Experience Cloud
site?
Should a Broker/Agency be able to complete a Life Insurance renewal on behalf of a customer?
Should a Broker/Agency be able to complete a mid-term adjustment for Life Insurance on behalf of a
customer?
Should a Broker/Agency have the ability to access Life Insurance documentation in the Experience
Cloud site?
Should a Broker/Agency have the ability to access Life Insurance commission statements in the
Experience Cloud site?
Should a Broker/Agency have access to Life Insurance commission structures in the Experience
Cloud site?
Should a Broker/Agency have access to Life Insurance commission sacrifice in the Experience Cloud
site?

Who will manage and define Dev-ops procedures for this implementation?
What pipeline tooling has been selected?
What source control tooling has been selected?
Is code quality tooling is being used?
Is the tooling integrated to the pipeline and dev tooling?
What tool will be used for Issue/User Story tracking?

Can orders be shipped independently at line item level?

Do you have the need to hold multiple versions of the plan because your customer sends you a

How many Engagement Programs would you like us to build?
Will you want us to enable Einstein Scoring for you?
Do you have a MCAE Sandbox we should use?
What are your deployment requirements?
What is the size of your database? How many email subscribers do you have?
Do you have any Business Units? If so, how many?
Do you have any additional connections you want set up?
Does the customer require SMS sending capabilities?
Does the customer require acquisition of a SMS short code?
How many keywords does the customer utilize?
Does the customer require Mobile Push capabilities? (Assumes customer will implement SDK with
guidance from Salesforce)
Does the customer require In App Messaging?
Does the customer require App Inbox Messaging?
Does the customer require Geofence Messaging?
Does the customer require Beacon Messaging?
Does the customer require Distributed Marketing? (If yes, assumes pre-requisite Marketing Cloud
configuration is in place or added via other scope items)
How many Quick Send use cases does the customer require?
How many Campaign Send use cases does the customer require?
How many Bulk Send use cases does the customer require?
Does the customer require custom Distributed Marketing Content Blocks?
Does the customer require content approval processes?
Does the customer have an internal change management process for rollout of Distributed Marketing
for CRM users? (If no training and change management should be scoped separately)
Does the customer require assistance with IP Warming?
How many IPs require warming support?
What is the customer's sending volume per month?
What level of intelligence does the customer have on subscriber engagement from their previous
ESP?
Does the customer require assistance setting up triggered sends?
How many simple complexity triggered sends? (Basic personalization only eg: First Name)
How many medium complexity triggered sends? (Mid complexity personalization eg: dynamic
content blocks)
How many high complexity triggered sends? (Complex personalization eg: looping rows)
Does the customer require assistance using Journey Builder?
Does the customer have a defined strategy for the use of Journey Builder?
How many simple complexity Journeys? (e.g. <5 touchpoints, simple injection criteria, email
channel only, minimal branching logic, no external integrations)

How many medium complexity Journeys? (e.g. 10+ touchpoints, moderate injection criteria, no more
than 2 communication channels, branching logic for some steps/touchpoints, SF data only required)
If so, how many high complexity Journeys? (e.g. 15+ touchpoints, complex injection criteria, omni-
channel, complex branching logic, multiple data streams)
Will the customer use the standard unsubscribe & preference centers or is a custom preference
center required?
If custom, does the preference center need to support multiple brands?
If custom, does the preference center need to be dynamic?
If custom, do the preferences need to sync back to an additional system of record?

Does the customer require multiple Business Units?
How many Business Units?
Do the different Business Units have different data sources and/or models?
Does the customer have multiple sending domains?
If so, how many different sending domains?
If so, do the different sending domains each require their own sending infrastructure (e.g. SAP)?
Does the customer require a dedicated testing Business Unit?
If so, does the testing Business Unit require external data connections? (e.g. connection to a
Sales/Service Cloud sandbox, an external data warehouse)
Does the customer require custom user profiles/permissions for MCE users?
If so, how many custom user profiles/permissions?
Does the customer require integration with other Salesforce Clouds?
If so, how many other Salesforce Clouds?
Does the customer require integration with Sales Cloud?
Does the customer require integration with Service Cloud?
Does the customer require integration with Commerce Cloud?
Does the customer require integration with Community Cloud?
Does the customer require integration with Data Cloud?
Does the customer require integration with Loyalty Cloud?
Does the customer have Salesforce Development/Administrator resources available?
Does the customer require integration with non-Salesforce data sources?
How many non-Salesforce data feeds are required to Marketing Cloud?
Do the non-Salesforce data sources have standard connectors?
Do the non-Salesforce data sources require API-based integration?
Do the non-Salesforce data sources have a unique & durable contact identifier?
How many data export processes are required from Marketing Cloud?
Does the customer require assistance with development of new email templates?
Does the customer require assistance with Static email creative? (Static emails with no dynamic
content or personalization in the email.)
Does the customer require assistance with Reusable Dynamic Content? (There is some dynamic
content but is reused in multiple emails e.g. first name, gender, language, etc.)
Does the customer require assistance with Custom Dynamic Content? (There is some dynamic
content that is not reused but is needed for variation. E.g. redemption banners, policy type, geo)
Does the customer require assistance with using a Modular Framework/Upload Method? (Builds are
created today through data tables that will populate all email content)
Does the customer require assistance with an email creative process more complex than all
previously mentioned?
How many existing email templates will the customer be migrating to Marketing Cloud? (Assumes
customer has available HTML and content)
How many Cloud Pages and forms will be configured in Marketing Cloud? (Assumes customer has
available HTML and content)
How many marketing intelligence use cases has the customer identified (e.g., Email Marketing
Optimization, Social Media Optimization, Paid Search Optimization, etc.)?
Is the customer a Publisher/Ad Seller?
Is the customer an agency serving multiple brands?
Is the customer an advertiser with multiple brands?
Is this engagement part of a larger multi-cloud deal?
Are reports required?

Loyalty Management

Voucher Management - Loyalty Management

How many reports will be implemented for this project? Most time-to-value projects will not include
report creation, unless the customer has a separate viz platform that requires such reports be
created.
Is connection to Tableau required?
Does this customer require Media Transparency Center for Ad Pacing/Reconciliation?
Does the customer require Granular Data Center (e.g., for Multi Touch Attribution data staging?)
How many harmonizations are expected with this implementation? NOTE: Most time-to-value
projects will have no more than 5-6 harmonizations. If you have more ask yourself why.
Is there intent to bring in historical data?
Does the historical data that will be implemented follow the same naming convention/taxonomy as
today's data?
Within each platform (e.g. Facebook Ads), how consistent are the naming conventions for Campaign
Name, Ad Set Name, etc.?
How many data sources will this implementation factor in? NOTE: Most time-to-value projects will
have no more than 8-12 data sources. If you have more ask yourself why.

How many search ads platforms (e.g., AdWords, Bing, Yahoo, etc.) will this implementation include?
How many social media paid ads platforms (e.g., Facebook Ads, LinkedIn Ads, Pinterest Ads, etc.)
will the implementation include?
How many organic social media platforms (e.g., Facebook Page Insights, Facebook Post Insights,
Pinterest Pins, LinkedIn Company Page, etc.) will the implementation include?
How many display or programmatic ads platforms (e.g., DCM, TheTradeDesk, Sizmek,
Flashtalking, etc.) will the implementation include?
How many video ads platforms (e.g., YouTube Ads, TikTok Ads, etc.) will the implementation
include?
Does the customer require custom connector development for any data sources? NOTE:
TotalConnect can ingest data via SFTP, email, or from a data warehouse, and therefore a customer
connector is typically unnecessary.
Which email marketing platform would be included in the implementation, if any?
Which web analytics platform would be included in the implementation, if any?
How many logins or sites for web analytics are needed for implementation?
How many non-digital media ads platforms (e.g., TV, Print, Radio, OOH) will the implementation
include?
Does the customer require CRM data as a data source for the marketing intelligence use case?
Are Dashboard pages required?

Are you using Salesforce Data Cloud for Loyalty Segmentation?
Do you any other segmentation tool that you want to use (Integrated with Loyalty Management) for

categories?

Do you give members points for purchases?

Qualifying and non-qualifying.
Are the points used to move members up or down Levels? (Qualifying Currency)

Are the points used to provide members vouchers based on milestones? (Non Qualifying Currency)
Can a member redeem the points in exchange for rewards from a catalogue? (Non Qualifying

For Loyalty points credit or for running promotions, do you want to create rules based on products and
product categories?

groups, etc.?

Do you want to set up Postpaid Billing for partners?

How many prior years of social and governance data are to be migrated?
How is asset and energy data currently managed?
How many source data systems of record will need to be drawn on?

Will any systems be directly integrated with Net Zero Cloud?
Who will develop integration code?
How do you roll up energy and carbon information from your assets?
How many facilities/buildings does your organization operate?
How are these facilities metered?
What forms of energy do you consume and track?
Do you track water consumption?
What proportion of your facilities' energy use do you have actual data for?
Does your organization operate a vehicle fleet?
What vehicle data do you track?
Do you track purchased goods and services?
Do you track purchased capital goods?
Do you track fuel and energy-related activities?
Do you track upstream transportation and distribution?
Do you track generated waste?
Do you track business travel?
Do you track employee commute?
Do you track upstream leased assets?
Do you track downstream transportation and distribution?
Do you track processing of sold products?
Do you track use of sold products?
Do you track end-of-life treatment for sold products?
Do you track downstream leased assets?
Do you track franchises?
Do you track financed emissions?
How will you obtain carbon emission data from your suppliers?
Do you have custom emission factors that you wish to use?
Do you purchase/retire carbon offsets or renewable energy instruments?
Do you have existing sustainability targets you wish to track with the platform?
Will you seek to leverage advanced forecasting functionality?
Will you leverage the Disclosure and Compliance Hub to produce report outputs (e.g. CDP)?
Will you seek to use the CRM Analytics dashboards?
Do you follow an application lifecycle management (ALM) process for Salesforce today?
Do you currently have a Salesforce Center of Excellence (COE)?
Do you track DevOps performance metrics?
How frequently do you release changes to the Salesforce system?

Do you have a change management process (including hotfixes and stakeholder communications)?
Do you have a branching strategy?
Do you have an environment strategy?
What level of testing/QA do you currently apply?
Do you use a CI tool?
Do you have any data deployment needs? This would be the use of Omnistudio datapacks, or CPQ
tools that use configuration as Salesforce records.
Do you have a sandbox strategy (refreshes, preview windows)?
How often do you refresh sandboxes?
Do you use a project management tool (Jira, Azure Boards etc.)?
Do you use a Git source control repository as source of truth?

Do you have a process to handle data migration and management during the Salesforce development
and release process?
Are there any specific security and compliance requirements your Salesforce implementation must
adhere to?
Do you have any established Salesforce development standards for all contributors to the codebase
e.g. test coverage, naming conventions?
Do you currently have internal or external Salesforce development teams?
How many Salesforce developers do you have (apex, lwc, js, automated testing etc.)?
How many click-based developers and admins do you have?
Do you have a release engineer or team?
Is this implementation being deployed to an existing Salesforce org?
How open is the customer to process optimization, etc. as part of this implementation engagement to
achieve "clicks not code"?
Does the customer have any data residency policies? (E.g. German Works Council, Russian data
residency, etc.)
Rate the level of customization required to implement the customer's requirements.
Will the solution require functionality that is not GA and is currently on the roadmap?
Does success of the engagement require the decommissioning of legacy systems?
Do we have adequate knowledge and understanding of the customer's technical architecture in the
org that the solution will reside in?
Does the customer have the required resources to support the implementation engagement?
Has the customer identified product owners for the implementation engagement?
Does the customer have dedicated teams aligned for supporting data migration for this
implementation engagement?
Is the customer knowledgeable in the clouds they intend to implement?
Will Subcontractors be required?
How many users will be deployed at the end of the program?
Does this implementation involve a global rollout? (Multiple countries)
Does this implementation involve multiple business units (LOBs)?
Has the customer done a change management assessment before starting this implementation
engagement?
Is the customer going to be loading large volumes of data on the platform?
Is the customer going to be doing high volume, real-time transactions? (TPS = transactions per
second)
Is data migration in scope for this implementation engagement?
Will middleware will be used for this implementation engagement and if so, what type?
Approximately how many Salesforce-integrated systems are going to impacted by this
implementation?
How mature is the customer's release management process? (communication, release triage, go/no-
go etc.)
Does the customer have a Change Management strategy?
What amount of pre-sales discovery has been completed?
Are the customer requirements well understood?
Is there a hard deadline that the customer needs to meet? (e.g. regulatory compliance, end of life of
current platform)
Does this implementation engagement have critical dependencies (e.g., regulatory, government,
other programs/projects, etc.) that are likely to inhibit success?
Have the Salesforce Professional Services Delivery and/or Practices teams been engaged in
discovery, scoping, or estimation?

Do you believe the customer's engagement timeline is aggressive?
Has the implementation scope, effort estimates, timeline or price been driven by customer budget
constraints?
How will the Salesforce solution be deployed for this Implementation?
Is this implementation engagement transformational?
Is there a C-level sponsor for the services project/program?
Is there alignment between the business and IT on the future state outcome?
Rate the customer's maturity on SCRUM/Agile/SAFE.
Will the engagement require access to any sensitive data (SSN, PII or PCI data)?
Does the customer have an acceptable DevOps process and tooling?
How would you rate the competency of the SI/Partner skills?
What level of governance does the customer have?
What is the reliance on partners for this implementation?
What is the customer's experience working with Salesforce Professional Services?
Is the implementation engagement addressing a failed SFDC project implementation?
Does the customer need Salesforce to provide Content Creators?
Does the customer need Salesforce to provide Instructors?
Does an existing PRM Experience Cloud site need to be updated alongside the release of Partner
Enablement?
Does the customer want to send email notifications to Partner Enablement users?
Does the customer want to configure In-App Guidance for partner users?
Does the customer expect Salesforce to provide PRM Enablement reports and dashboards specific
to their use-cases?
Does the customer expect Salesforce to setup Enablement permission sets?
Should the Enablement site reflect the customer's branding?
Does the customer need Salesforce to setup the Connected App for Enablement?
Does the customer need Salesforce to define access to the Enablement site Content Collections?
Does the customer want to add custom navigation elements to their Enablement site? (eg.
customer's help site, logout url, site footer)
Does the customer need Salesforce support to define content filters?
Does the customer want to enable Badge Ratings? (module feedback)
Does the customer need Salesforce aid to setup a sandbox for the Enablement site?
Does the customer want to define custom Measures?
Is Sales one of the personas who will use the Enablement site?
Will the customer be using the Sales Enablement Program templates?
Is Service one of the personas who will use the Enablement site?
Will the customer be using the Service Enablement Program template?
Does the customer have additional unique use-cases that will have to be configured from scratch?
Does the customer want to configure In-App Guidance?

Does the customer expect Salesforce to provide reports and dashboards specific to their use-cases?
Does the customer need automation for Enablement assignment?
Does the customer need Salesforce to define and/or implement Learning Paths?
Does the customer require help defining their enablement site url? (subdomain)
Has the customer identified the business outcomes they want to drive through Enablement?
Has the customer identified their Enablement team? (subject matter experts, content creators,
release managers, administrators, etc.)
Does the customer require help defining their Enablement release process and cadence?
Is the Enablement site intended to be used by internal users, external users, or both?

Does the customer need access to Salesforce on Mobile?
How many personas require Mobile access?
Are users expected to access the application via mobile while disconnected from the internet? (work
offline)
Where/how will leads be captured? (Standard Web-to-Lead)
How will Leads be assigned?
Are notifications necessary when new leads come in? If so, by email or chatter?
Is there already a Lead Qualification process with stages in place?
Does the customer need to identify duplicate Leads converging from multiple sources? (website, list
purchases, event registrations, etc.)
Does the customer intend to forecast Opportunities and/or Product Families by Territories?
Is data migration expected?
How many objects expected to be migrated?
What is the estimated volume of records to be migrated?
Should Orders be visible (read-only and mastered externally) on the Account in Salesforce?
Should Invoices be visible (read-only and mastered externally) on the Account in Salesforce?
Should email be synced with Salesforce?
What is the expected number of unique email templates (configured in Salesforce) that should be
available to the Sales Team?
Should Salesforce Inbox be configured?
How complex is the customer's account segmentation process? (# of criteria considered for
segmentation)
Are there relationships between some of the Accounts?
How many currencies should be supported?
How often is the currency(ies) updated?
Are the currencies mastered in an external system? (e.g. ERP system)
Should Sales Contracts be created in Salesforce?
What is the system of record for Contacts?
How many different Contact types is the customer working with? (That have a need for different
views and data collected)
Can Contacts be associated to more than one Account?
Is there a need to track or prevent the creation of duplicate Contacts?
Should Sales Reps' sales incentives be visible/available in Salesforce?
Will Chatter be used on Accounts, Contacts, and Opportunities?
Is there a need to use Campaigns to track engagement and participation in marketing efforts?
Is there a need to track Campaign Influence across Contacts, Campaigns, and Opportunities?
Does the customer do Account Planning?

How complex is Account Planning in the company? (Simple: Sales reps work only on calendar
events, tasks and notes or Advanced: Sales reps work on Shared Wallets, Product line planning, etc.)
Who are the customer's customers?
Who will own the Accounts in the system?
How should the Account visibility/access be defined?
Are different screens required depending on the account type, user region, or any other
categorization?
Is the Account master data managed in Salesforce or in an external system? (e.g. ERP system)
Will Accounts be created in Salesforce and distributed to other systems?
How many personas are involved in Account Management? (e.g. Master data team, Sales Rep,
Sales Engineer)

Is the current Account process customized in Salesforce? (If the customer is already using the
solution)
Is there a need to track or prevent the creation of duplicate Accounts?
Is Account information verified in an external system? (e.g. Experian)
Does the customer purge their Account data into a data warehouse or an external database? If so,
how often does this process take place?
What is the strategy for training on Sales Engagement (HVS)? Do they need any assistance with
this?
Will Sales Engagement (HVS) out-of-the-box Reports & Dashboards meet your needs or will you
need custom Reports & Dashboards?
Will the customer leverage Outlook Integration?
Will the customer leverage Inbox?
How many users will Sales Engagement (HVS) be rolled out for?
What are the expected timelines for go live for each team?

Are the users located in a different geographical location than the planned SF implementation team?
Will this be rolled out for English-speaking users only? If not, what other languages will need to be
supported?
Will Sales Engagement (HVS) be used for leads, contacts and/or person accounts?
A target running in a sales cadence can associate to ONE opportunity. Will you require all users to
Relate Opportunities to Sales Cadences?
Expected volumes of active targets?
How many sales cadences need to be built for phase 1? (Expect an answer by each team.)
How many environments will Sales Engagement (HVS) need to be deployed in? (sandboxes and
prod counts)
Will you allow for users to change cadence assignee? If so, will you limit who can change target
assignees by perm set?
Will you enable allow email template and call script variant testing?
Will you enable Skip Weekends? (Assign sales cadence tasks to reps on weekdays only.)
Will Sales Engagement (HVS) need to integrate with external systems? (IE: There are linked in sales
navigator steps that can be leveraged)

Do you already have Enhanced Email enabled? If not, reviewing the considerations might surface
customization items. More information here:
https://help.salesforce.com/s/articleView?language=en_US&type=5&id=sf.emailadmin_enhanced_e
mail_considerations.htm
Expected volumes of manual email?
Will you enable email tracking? If so, need IPs to exclude for internal activity.
Expected volumes of automatic email?
Will new email templates need to be created?
Who owns the email template creation?
Will ECI (Einstein Conversion Insights) be leveraged?
Does the CTI solution integrate with ECI?
Will the customer leverage Einstein Activity Capture?
What CTI solution will be leveraged for Sales Engagement (HVS) users?
Is the CTI solution already rolled out?
Is the CTI solution already integrated with Sales Engagement (HVS)? If not, there is a workaround for
advancing the sales cadence and branching.

Will there be any specific scope needed for change management for this product? NOTE: This
question relates only to Sales Engagement HCC expected functions to be carried out by SF. If part of
a larger change with this implementation, these questions are more adequately covered at a macro
level in the Engagement Non-Functional Reqs Q&A section of this Scoping Assessment.
Will you leverage call branches in sales cadences? If so, are the call results defined enough that they
can map to the sales cadence call disposition results for mapping?
Will Sales Engagement (HVS) be used to support inbound, outbound calls or both?
Will new call scripts templates need to be created?
Who owns the call script creation?
What are the key objectives you want to accomplish as an outcome of this implementation?
What are the business problems/pain points you want to address?
Do you plan to use out-of-the-box (OOTB) features or do you need additional automations or custom
development?
Will you require custom User Interface components or use the OOTB UI?
How many countries and regions will be modeled?
What will be the source for the datasets?
What is the combined record size of the datasets?
How many alignments are expected average per model?
Where the chosen model will be published to?
Does publishing require approval?
What is the expected continual territory optimization frequency?
How many address information field sets are intended to be used as base objects for geocoding?
Do you need integration with custom internal and external apps?
Do you plan to use desktop, mobile or both?
How many roles in teams will use Maps (permission groups)?
Which target object are you using for calendar?
How many visit plans with different datasets do you need?
Is the rep assignment based on the base or related object?
Are visit windows fixed or variable (advanced)?
Do you have Promotional Windows?
Will you be using automated messages in Whatsapp?
How many automated message templates will you be creating?
Do you plan to use Facebook to create cases?
Do you plan to use Twitter to create cases?
Do you plan to use Instagram to create cases?
Do you plan to use YouTube to create cases?
Do you plan to use (Sina) Weibo to create cases?
Will a new number need to be provisioned or will you be using an existing number?
What number needs to be provisioned by Salesforce?
Which country should the number be provisioned for?
How many numbers need to be provisioned?
Are there auto responses that need to be supported in SMS?
Will you need to broadcast SMS to multiple contacts?
What kind of messages will need to be supported in the channel?
Will you need reports/ dashboards built to support channel related metrics?
How should incoming work be accepted by an agent?
How should the supervisor monitor agents?
Where will the chat button be hosted?
Do you plan to have 24x7 support or set Business Hours?

Are Sitemaps needed to expose relevant content to public search engines?
Do we need to configure Robot.txt file to ensure right pages from knowledge portal are exposed to
public search engines?
Do we need to configure Article meta tags on knowledge portal to help search engines with
appropriate search results?
Will the development of Salesforce Knowledge custom reports to track how articles are created,
maintained, and delivered be needed?
Are analytics to track page views on public site be needed? If so, what type?
Do you need to enable the "Compare Versions of an Article" component for agents?
Are we replacing an existing knowledge base or building out a new one?
How many article types are needed(e.g., FAQs, How-to guides)?
For the Knowledge Article migration, is the source another Salesforce org or a non-Salesforce
source?
For Knowledge Migration from a non-Salesforce source, is the source data structured or
unstructured?
What is the volume of Knowledge Articles that need to be migrated?
Do the Article versions also needs to be migrated?
Do the Article views need to be migrated?
Do the Article ratings need to be migrated?
Do the Articles have file attachments?
Do the Articles have Rich Text Content like images and videos in their body?
Do the Articles have translations that needs to be migrated? (multilingual knowledge base)
Do the Articles have embedded links to other articles in them? Does it need to be preserved?
Do the Knowledge Articles need to be in sync with any other system? For example, should the source
not be deleted and need to be updated when knowledge is created or updated in Salesforce?
Is there a need for any unauthenticated Rest endpoints to fetch public knowledge content? (Use case:
other systems in your org want to access knowledge content for their applications.)
Do you need any authenticated REST endpoints to fetch both public and internal knowledge content?
(Use case: other systems/teams in your org want to access internal knowledge content for their
applications.)
Do you need to integrate with external systems or other knowledge sources for Knowledge Article
creation or adding references to knowledge articles? (e.g: Knowledge articles needs to be associated
with a JIRA ticket, will need integration with JIRA)
Are any two-way integrations needed between Knowledge and external systems?
Are there custom fields or attributes you would like to associate with curated articles? For example,
do you need custom fields on articles to capture additional details like Internal Notes, Product Bug#,
etc.?
Is multilingual support required for curated articles? How should translations be managed?
What will be the Article Publishing Lifecycle?
Article Feedback Management: Does all feedback need to be tracked and actioned upon? NOTE:
Only Product experts can give feedback or is it open for everyone including public users as well.
Are there specific automation requirements for article creation, updates, or retirement?
Would you like to incorporate AI-driven content creation for article curation?
How will the Knowledge sidebar in the Agent Console be deployed?
How will Consolidated Search across different knowledge sources (within Salesforce and outside
Salesforce) be handled for agents?
Does the customer want the ability to create Knowledge articles from cases?
Ability to associate Knowledge articles to cases: Is it mandatory to have a knowledge article
associated to a case before closing the case? (validation rules/flows needs to be in place)

Resource Management - Salesforce Field Service

Time Reporting - Is time registration needed?
Expense Reporting - Do field resources need to create Expenses?
Service Reports - Do field resources generate documents generated after work closure?
Geolocation and Route guidance - Does resource location need to be tracked and routing information
provided?
Geolocation-Based Actions - Do actions need to be automated based on the location of field
resources?
Ad-hoc Work Creation - Do field resources need to create work orders?
Resource Self-Scheduling - Do field resources need to reschedule work?
Global Search - Does field resource need to be able to search for records?
Time Reporting - Are payrolls calculations in scope?
Is Mobile in scope?
Salesforce Field Service (Android) - Please describe the mobile devices that are being used by the
resources? Do resources work also from a laptop?
Salesforce Field Service (iOS) - Please describe the mobile devices that are being used by the
resources? Do resources work also from a laptop?
Offline - Should the Mobile solutions offer offline functionality?
Is Knowledge Management in scope?
Do resources need to access knowledge articles while performing interventions on-site?
Describe the lifecycle of a knowledge article.
Is multi-language support required for Knowledge?
Pick the correct article taxonomy:
How are articles assigned to work order and service appointments?
Is Work Order Management in scope?
Work Order Definition - What types of work needs to be scheduled?
Long Term Work Order Management - Will work orders span across multiple weeks/months?
Work Order Lifecycle - How complex is the WO/SA status model?
Work Completion & Approval - Are there additional approval steps required before work items can be
completed?
Is Fleet Management & Tracking in scope?
Vehicle Maintenance - How important is it for your operations to minimize vehicle downtime, and
what challenges do you currently face in keeping your fleet well-maintained?
Vehicle Tracking - How critical is it for your team to know the real-time location of vehicles, and how
do you currently manage vehicle routing and dispatch efficiency?
Vehicle Safety - What are the most important safety concerns for your fleet, and how do you ensure
that both vehicles and drivers adhere to safety standards?
Tools Management & Tracking - How do you currently manage the availability and location of tools
and equipment, and what issues have you encountered with tool loss or inefficiencies?
Tools Management & Tracking - Do you have tools that are scarce and must be pre-booked to be
available for a specific job?
Is Resource Management in scope?
Territory Management - Are the resources expected to manage multiple territories?
Skill & Qualification Management - Do the resources have skills assigned that are used for
scheduling?
Resource Coverage - Are there challenges with sufficient or balanced skilled resources available
across all service areas?

Resource Availability - How is the individual resource availability (operating hours) controlled?
Shift Management - Do resources work based on shifts instead of static availabilities?
Absence Management - Will resource absences be reflected in Salesforce?
Crew Management - Do resources work in crews?
Time Approval - Does resource work time needs to be approved by a manager?
Skill & Qualification Management - Is resource efficiency factor being used?
Is Field Service Analytics in scope?
Reports & Dashboards - Is field service reporting needed?
Is Customer Engagement in scope?
Customer Notifications - How do you notify your customers about updates on their appointments?
Self-Service Scheduling - Will customer appointments be booked from an external systems e.g web
portal?
(Contactless) Signature Capture - Do you need to let your customers use their own device instead of
the Field Service mobile worker's device to digitally sign service reports?
Customer Feedback & Surveys - Do you capture your customers feedback?
Is Scheduling in scope?
Schedule Planning (manual) - Will the resources have more than one break during the day (e.g.
lunch, tea)?
Schedule Planning (manual) - Are there different groups of resources who require different durations
of lunch break?
Schedule Planning (manual) - Should work be assigned to named resources?
Schedule Planning (semi-automatic) - Do you expect the schedule to be automatically adjusted based
on the updates from service resource mobile app?
Schedule Optimization - Is work expected to be automatically assigned to the resources?
Schedule Optimization - Is reactive work created on the day of service and is it expected to be
scheduled automatically?
Schedule Optimization - How many schedulable resources are being licensed?
Appointment Booking - Are the customer provided fixed time slots (appointments) for service?
Appointment Reschedule - How do you handle rescheduling requests?
Resource Self-Scheduling - Do resources schedule appointments to themselves?
Jeopardy Management - Will the dispatchers require alerts to manage the scheduling exceptions?
Emergency Scheduling - Do you have emergency scheduling, where you need to immediately
schedule a service?
Backlog Management - How do you prioritize appointments?
Schedule Rollover - How do you want overlaps to be handled?
Multi-Day Work - Do the jobs have a duration greater than 8 hours?
Appointment Bundling - Will the resources complete more than 6 jobs in a day?
Same-Site work - Do you try to ensures that a minimal number of mobile workers visit the same
location? (e.g. If there is work in same building but different customers, would you send one worker
or many workers?)
Work Dependencies - Are there jobs that are dependant on other jobs (same start, one after the other,
etc.)?
Dispatch Management - How should the work be dispatched to resources?
Forecasting & Capacity Planning - Do the planners need reports to forecast job volumes and plan in
advance?
Field Resource Monitoring - Do you track your workers location?
Is Cross-Sell/Up-sell in scope?
Guided Selling - Will the field resource need a guided selling process?
Payment - is on-site payment required?

Onboarding & Qualification - Can you describe the process for Qualifying and Onboarding
Contractors? How much of this process is expected to be managed within Salesforce Field Service?
Assignment & Scheduling - Please describe how work is scheduled to Contractors and how
Contractors execute the work? Do you need to be able to schedule work to individual contractors or
do Contractor companies schedule the work themselves?
Assignment & Scheduling - Do contractors need to have access to SFS Mobile App, simple web-
based UI or use their existing systems?
Communication & Collaboration - What are the essential communication methods and tools that
contractors and internal teams need to effectively collaborate?

Performance Monitoring and Reporting - What key metrics and performance indicators are important
to track for contractors, and how often do you need to review and report on this performance?

Contract & Payment Management - How varied are the contract types and payment terms for
contractors, and how important is it to ensure that payments are processed accurately and on time?
Integration with Other Systems - What other systems (e.g., finance, HR) need to work closely with
the contractor management process, and how important is it that information flows smoothly between
them? Do contractors use their own systems for scheduling and executing the work?
Is Contract, Entitlement, Warranty Management in scope?
Contract Management - Where are contracts mastered?
Entitlement Management - Do you need to check customers entitlements for services before
dispatching a resource or worker to do the job?
Service-Level Agreement (SLA) Management - Do you have obligations to customers to start
specific jobs in x amount of time or during specific hours?
Warranty Management - Do you need to check if a service is included within the warranty and record
labor, parts and expenses along with any exchange options that are provided to rectify issues with
products sold or installed?
Is Field Inventory Management in scope?
Managing Required Parts - do you have parts assigned as required to job types?
Ordering Parts - Will back-office personnel request/order parts?
Van Stock - Do you need to track Van Stock?
Transfering Parts - Will users transfer parts between locations (Vans/Warehouses)?
Receiving Parts - Will resources confirm receipt of parts?
Consuming Parts - Will resources consume parts?
Returning Parts - Will there be a parts return process?
Inventory Replenishment - Will resource inventory be replenished?
Serialized Inventory - Will some parts be serialized?
Batch-managed Inventory - Will some parts need to have a batch number recorded for them?
Van Stock - Is resource inventory stocked in a van location associated to their service resource
record?
Warehouse Stock - Is inventory stocked in a warehouse location?
Warehouse Management - Will inventory be managed at warehouse location(s)?
Depot Repair - Will parts be sent to a depot for repair work?

Return Merchandise Authorization (RMA) - Will there be a need for an RMA process?
Product Pricing - Will pricing of products be used in Salesforce?
Product Substitution - Will there be suggested product substitutions for unavailable products?
Is Asset Management in scope?
Asset Data Model - How complex is your Asset Hierarchy?
Reactive Maintenance - Do you have reactive Maintenance?
Preventative Maintenance - Do you have Preventive Maintenance with recurring appointments at a
customers site?
Predictive Maintenance - Do you have Predictive Maintenance? (Asset data gets updated by IoT)
Asset Install Management - What happens when you install an asset at a customer?
Service History - Does your worker need to see the service history of an asset and or service history
on a customers site while on the job?
Who will use a Service Cloud console in your organization? What are the different user types?
Do different user groups need to see different information in the console?
Do you want console users to connect with customers or website visitors in real time through text-
only live chats?
Do you want to customize, extend, or integrate a Service Cloud console with other systems?
Does the customer have a use case/ business need to use Chat Bots?
Are any of the channels being used Email, Chat, Social, Mobile,etc?
How many queues in each channel?
Will be using queues or skill based routing/how is it structured today for existing agents and
processes?
Do you have blended agents?
Are you looking for external routing rules/engine?
Is one of the channels Phone?
Is one of the channels Email?
Is one of the channels Social?
Is one of the channels Chat?
Is one of the channels Mobile?
Within a customer support group, how are the teams structured (by skillset, product, location,
activity, etc.)?
Are the customers processes documented?
Are there any restrictions on visibility of Cases, Accounts/Contacts or Service Contracts?
Are there any compliance or quality considerations?
Are the reporting requirements complex or simple?
How many call centers do you manage?
Where are all call centers located?
How do agents communicate internally (other agents, managers, etc.)?
Are your customers businesses, individual consumers, or both?
Are there any Reports or Dashboards that have to be shared with Agents/Reps regarding any
changes in AHT, Case Status, Prioritization, Case Queues, etc.?
Do any of the reports or dashboards require data from external systems? If yes, will the SF
Implementation team be responsible for building any of these data feeds and how frequently do they
need to run?
Will the customer enable the following channel: Voice
Will the customer enable the following channel: Email
Will the customer enable the following channel: Web
Will the customer enable the following channel: Text
Will the customer enable the following channel: Social

How many web properties do you plan to include chat?
Are there different chat teams who support different product/service areas? What are they?
Do you plan to use proactive chat/invites? If so what are the rules?
Will you authenticate users from Service Cloud Portal or unauthenticated from Sites?
How many cases do you expect from Chat?
How many chats can an agent support simultaneously?
Do chat agents have specific business hours?
Does your support require routing of Chat to Agents with specific product knowledge?
Do you have canned messaging you would like your Agents to use? If so, how many?
Will you use Knowledge in the Service Cloud deployment and will you want to attach Knowledge in
Live Agent?
Is there any type of info you would like to collect before a chat is initiated?
If so, do you use any information in the prechat form to match a customer?
Will chat windows need to be branded?
What happens after a chat is done? Will post-chat surveys be utilized?
How many steps are there in your post chat survey?
Do you allow agents transfer chat to a different agent?
How many call center agents do you have?
Does the customer have a use case/ business need to use Live Messages?
Are they replacing an existing knowledge base or is this a new venture for them?
How many article types will they need?
What is the publication process?
How many channels will knowledge be available for?
Do they have any transclusion requirements?
Will synonyms be used?
Do you plan to import existing articles?
How many languages do you need articles in?
What is the estimated volume of knowledge articles, existing systems and platforms used to manage
knowledge articles?
Can customers rank the articles?
Can customers comment on the articles?
How often are legacy articles deleted?
Are there any legal/compliance requirements to retain content? If so, for how long?
Do archived or deleted articles need to be retained in Salesforce for compliance reasons, or will they
be stored externally? (Discover/determine the external system.)
Are the existing 3 Data Categories and 50 sub-categories sufficient to address business needs?
Depending on the content migration volume, do we need assistance from other applications?
Do agents comment/provide feedback on articles, other than rating them?
Does the client have a use case for Knowledge Management?
Which tool is needed?
Do you solicit feedback?
How are customers selected for feedback/VOC?
Do you have different survey process for different service processes?
How do you solicit or plan to solicit feedback from the customer?
Do you have customer specific entitlement processes along with associated SLAs?
Do you have all the entitlement related data stored in Salesforce?
Are there SLAs based on the Case Type?
What happens if an SLA is breached?
If you have multiple entitlements, how does an agent know which one to apply?

How are milestones completed?
Do you need the system to conditionally auto-assign the entitlement to cases?
How many business hours/shifts do you have for your agents?
Do you have named customers that are allowed to contact support?
What information do you need to provide back to your customers about support?
How many channels would you support with Bot for this implementation phase? e.g, Chat, SMS,
FBM, ABC, Wechat, Whatsapp?
For each of the channel mentioned, do you have support by agent currently?
If you do not have agent support this channel, would you include the channel implementation for
agents in this project? (Note: from UX perspective we recommend having agents backup bot if
possible.)
Is agent offline support process/requirement, if you run bot 24x7, or enabling a bot in a channel without
agents?
What's the bot use case? Is this primarily a FAQ bot or transaction based bot such as checking order
status?
For each of the required transactions, does it require integration?
For transaction/personalized experience, do you plan to verify customer?
Do you want to implement NLP at launch? Or is menu based bot is acceptable at launch?
For a FAQ type of bot, would it require integration to knowledge base or only a few articles that can be
built into the bot itself?
What metrics would you like to capture to measure success of the Bot deployment?
How many bot sessions on a daily basis do you expect at launch?
If Bot to agent escalation happens, does it go to the same group of agents or information provided to
bot may direct the routing to different agent group? If so, what are the requirements?
Is there a pre-chat requirement?
How is work routed today?
Are agents cross-skilled? Do they also do just that work or any other filler work to keep agent
Utilization and occupancy high?
Are agents dedicated to an activity/skill?
What kind of reporting is done on interactions/cases?
Is there an EOD (end of day) or other interval, showing volume or cases/interactions with other
metrics such as Average Speed of Answer (ASA), Average Handle time (AHT), Average Talk Time
(ATT), After Call Work (ACW)?
Do agents get access to their metrics so that they can see their performance results?
What data is exposed on this report? KPIs/SLAs? CTI and telephony metrics?
Do we share case data with customer - what can be shared and how?
Escalation reporting? How is that managed and is there a need to pursue a solution?
Which phone system are you using today?
How many call centers do you have? If there are multiple call centers, are they all using the same
phone system or different ones? If different, please specify
Is Service Cloud Console going to be used?
What data should an agent see when a call comes in? Any data coming in from external systems?
Do you have an IVR with any data that is passed to the agent beyond the ANI?
Do you have international agents where there could be network reliability or performance issues?
Do managers shadow calls?
Do you need call recording to be exposed on case?
How many records / data entities are likely to be shown to the agent on receiving a call?
Do you do phone based post call surveys?
Does the client have a use case for CTI Voice?
